[EVENT: IRQ49793]
Date: 2023-11-08
Country: Iraq | Location: Basrah
Type: Battles > Armed clash | Disorder: Political violence
Actor1: Peace Companies [Political militia]

Fatalities: 3
Sources: Al Mirbad; Al Sumaria TV; Rudaw; Shafaaq News [National]

DESCRIPTION:

The security forces closed off several intersections on the airport road to contain the fighting. Asaib and Peace Companies militants also clashed in the Hayyana neighborhood. Iraqi security forces sustained 3 injured. the clashes resulted in 2 fatalities from Asaib and 1 fatality from Peace Companies. In total, the clash resulted in 3 fatalities and 4 injured.
